## Chunk 9715: DTC P0716 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0716 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12321.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_7f7364146c90`
- Images: `images\GHH399820.jpeg`, `images\GHH399821.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19450.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0716 (K20C2)

DTC P0716: Input/Turbine Speed Sensor "A" Circuit Range/Performance

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The torque converter turbine speed sensor is attached to the outside of the transmission housing. The torque converter turbine speed sensor detects the number of revolutions of the gear on the input shaft and sends a pulsing signal to the transmission control module (TCM). The TCM converts the pulses to torque converter turbine speed. If no pulses occur with the input shaft rotating, the TCM detects a malfunction that may be caused by an open, an intermittent open, or a short to ground. Based on the correlation between the torque converter turbine speed sensor and the CVT drive pulley speed sensor, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 10 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 550 rpm | -

Torque converter turbine speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

Other | Not when the shift lever is being shifted

No electrical noise in the torque converter turbine speed sensor output circuit

When the accelerator pedal is pressed

[ ]:HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The rotation speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] measured by the torque converter turbine speed sensor is less than the rotation speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] measured by the CVT drive pulley speed sensor by a factor of 0.56 for at least 10 seconds.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor NT line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor NT line short to ground

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Drive the vehicle at a speed of 19 mph (30 km/h) or more for at least 30 seconds.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
- Torque converter turbine speed sensor NT line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor VCC line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor SG line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor NT line short to ground

- Torque converter turbine speed sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Drive the vehicle at a speed of 19 mph (30 km/h) or more for at least 30 seconds.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9719: DTC P0741 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0741 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12325.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_3106b6454f3d`
- Images: `images\GHH399828.jpeg`, `images\GHH399829.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19454.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0741 (K20C2)

DTC P0741: Torque Converter Clutch Circuit Performance or Stuck OFF

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The power transfer capacity of the torque converter clutch is controlled by the balance of transmission fluid supply to and discharge from the torque converter. When hydraulic pressure is applied internally, the torque converter clutch turns ON, and when hydraulic pressure is applied from the back pressure side, the torque converter clutch turns OFF. As the hydraulic pressure from the internal pressure side increases, the power transfer capacity of the torque converter clutch increases. The direction of hydraulic pressure supply is switched by shift solenoid valve B and the LC shift valve. Transmission fluid is supplied from the internal pressure side to shift solenoid valve B when the signal from the transmission control module (TCM) is ON (12 V), and transmission fluid is supplied from the back pressure side when it is OFF (0 V). The balance of internal pressure and back pressure is controlled by the CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve and the LC control valve. The CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve maximizes the power transfer capacity of the torque converter clutch when the signal from the TCM is ON (1 A), and it minimizes the power transfer capacity of the torque converter clutch when the signal from the TCM is OFF (0 A). If the ratio of engine speed and input shaft speed is not about 1:1 while the TCM is issuing the command to turn shift solenoid valve B and the CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve ON, the TCM detects a faulty lock-up control system and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 30 seconds or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 1, 000 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | 28 mph (45 km/h) | -

Accelerator pedal position variation | - | 13 deg./1 second

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

Difference between target torque converter slip rate and actual torque converter slip rate is more than 15 % for at least 30 seconds or more.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Torque converter clutch failure

- CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve stuck off

- Shift solenoid valve B stuck off

- LC shift valve stuck

- LC control valve stuck

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever from N position/mode to D position/mode.

- Drive the vehicle at a steady speed of 37 mph (60 km/h), for at least 1 minute.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 4.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P0746 (K20C2)

DTC P0746: CVT Drive Pulley Pressure Control Solenoid Valve Stuck OFF

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the drive pulley. The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. To shift the transmission properly, the transmission control module (TCM) outputs a drive pulley hydraulic pressure control signal. If the pulley ratio is overdrive while the vehicle is stopped with the forward clutch engaged, and the estimated input torque when starting is a specified value or more and belt slippage does not occur when driving, the TCM detects a CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck OFF failure and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | Depending on the driving conditions

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed]* | - | 3 mph (5 km/h)

Accelerator pedal position | - | 2.7 deg.*

4 deg.** | -

30 deg.*** | -

Shift lever position/mode | D

*: Symptom 1

**: Symptom 2-A

***: Symptom 2-B

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

Symptom 1, Symptom 2-A, and Symptom 2-B occurs.

Symptom | Vehicle conditions | TCM detection

1 | Stopped with forward clutch engaged | Pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is more than 0.3, or less than 0.6 for at least 2.5 seconds

2 | 2-A | Starting | Excessive input torque

2-B | Running | Pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is less than 3.0

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck off

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- Accelerate the vehicle in D position/mode until the speed reaches 37 mph (60 km/h).

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Accelerate the vehicle with wide open throttle.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 5.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9727: DTC P0792 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0792 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12333.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_b3660da36fb2`

````text
# DTC P0792 (K20C2)

DTC P0792: Intermediate Shaft Speed Sensor "A" Circuit Range/Performance

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT drive pulley speed sensor is attached to the outside of the transmission housing. The CVT drive pulley speed sensor detects the number of revolutions of the gear on the drive pulley and sends a pulsing signal to the transmission control module (TCM). The TCM converts the pulses to drive pulley speed. If no pulses occur with the drive pulley rotating, the TCM detects a malfunction that may be caused by an open, an intermittent open, or a short to ground. Based on the correlation between the CVT drive pulley speed sensor and the torque converter turbine speed sensor, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 10 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 500 rpm | -

Torque converter turbine speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] | 550 rpm | -

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

Other | Not when the shift lever is being shifted

No electrical noise in the CVT drive pulley speed sensor output circuit

Accelerator pedal pressed

[ ]:HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The rotation speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] measured by the CVT drive pulley speed sensor is less than the rotation speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] measured by the torque converter turbine speed sensor by a factor of 0.53 for at least 10 seconds.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT drive pulley speed sensor NDR-PWM line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- CVT drive pulley speed sensor NDR-PWM line short to ground

- CVT drive pulley speed sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Drive the vehicle at a speed of 19 mph (30 km/h) or more for at least 30 seconds.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P0792 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

DTC P0792: Intermediate Shaft Speed Sensor "A" Circuit Range/Performance

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT drive pulley speed sensor is attached to the outside of the end cover. The CVT drive pulley speed sensor detects the number of revolutions of the gear on the drive pulley and sends a pulsing signal to the transmission control module (TCM). The TCM converts the pulses to drive pulley speed. If no pulses occur with the drive pulley rotating, the TCM detects a malfunction that may be caused by an open, an intermittent open, or a short to ground. Based on the correlation between the CVT drive pulley speed sensor and the torque converter turbine speed sensor, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 10 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 500 rpm | -

Torque converter turbine speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] | 550 rpm | -

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

Shift lever position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

Other | Not when the shift lever is being shifted

No electrical noise in the CVT drive pulley speed sensor output circuit

Accelerator pedal pressed

[ ]:HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The rotation speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] measured by the CVT drive pulley speed sensor is less than the rotation speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] measured by the torque converter turbine speed sensor by a factor of 0.53 for at least 10 seconds.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT drive pulley speed sensor NDR-PWM line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- CVT drive pulley speed sensor NDR-PWM line short to ground

- CVT drive pulley speed sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Drive the vehicle at a speed of 19 mph (30 km/h) or more for at least 30 seconds.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9731: DTC P0796 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0796 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12337.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_d8bbd4098b41`
- Images: `images\GHH399850.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19466.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0796 (K20C2)

DTC P0796: Pressure Control Solenoid Valve "C" Performance or Stuck OFF

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the forward clutch and the reverse brake. The CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. To supply proper forward clutch/reverse brake hydraulic pressure, the transmission control module (TCM) outputs a CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve hydraulic pressure control signal. If the torque converter turbine speed and drive pulley speed does not match when forward clutch/reverse brake is engaged at a specified vehicle speed condition, the TCM detects a CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve stuck OFF failure and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 30 seconds or less

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Forward clutch/reverse brake commanded pressure | 206 kPa (2.1 kgf/cm 2, 29.9 psi) | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | - | 1 mph (3 km/h)

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L, R

With paddle shifter | D, S, R

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The rotation speed between the torque converter turbine speed [Torque Converter Turbine Speed] and the drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] is 250 rpm or more.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve stuck off

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever from N position/mode to D position/mode, and wait for at least 30 seconds.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 2.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P0797 (K20C2)

DTC P0797: Pressure Control Solenoid Valve "C" Stuck ON

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the forward clutch and the reverse brake. The CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. To supply proper forward clutch/reverse brake hydraulic pressure, the transmission control module (TCM) outputs a CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve hydraulic pressure control signal. If the duration of the forward clutch/reverse brake engage operation from start to completion is short, the TCM detects a CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve stuck ON failure and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | Depending on the driving conditions

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | - | 1 mph (3 km/h)

Accelerator pedal position | - | 0.6 deg.

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

Forward clutch engagement when shifted from N position/mode to D position/mode takes less than 0.4 second.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve stuck on

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever from N position/mode to D position/mode, then shift to N position/mode, and wait for at least 2 seconds.

- Repeat Driving Pattern step 2, five times.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 3.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9735: DTC P0842, P0843 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0842, P0843 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12341.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_e86ea2c107ad`
- Images: `images\GHH399854.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19470.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0842, P0843 (K20C2)

DTC P0842: Transmission Fluid Pressure Sensor/Switch "A" Circuit Low

DTC P0843: Transmission Fluid Pressure Sensor/Switch "A" Circuit High

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT driven pulley pressure sensor uses a strain gauge. The output voltage changes depending on the driven pulley pressure. The transmission control module (TCM) sends a 5 V reference voltage to the sensor through a pull-up resistor. When the driven pulley pressure is low, the sensor resistance decreases and the TCM detects a low signal voltage. Conversely, when the driven pulley pressure is high, the sensor resistance increases and the TCM detects a high signal voltage. If the CVT driven pulley pressure sensor signal voltage is a specified voltage for a set time, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 10 seconds or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition

State of the engine | Running

Malfunction Threshold

DTC: P0842

The CVT driven pulley pressure sensor output voltage [Driven Pulley Pressure Sensor(V)] is less than 0.21 V for at least 10 seconds.

DTC: P0843

The CVT driven pulley pressure sensor output voltage [Driven Pulley Pressure Sensor(V)] is more than 4.84 V for at least 10 seconds.

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

DTC: P0842

- CVT driven pulley pressure sensor PDN line short to ground

DTC: P0843

- CVT driven pulley pressure sensor PDN line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- CVT driven pulley pressure sensor SG line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

Common

- CVT driven pulley pressure sensor VCC line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- CVT driven pulley pressure sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Shift to P position/mode, and wait for at least 10 seconds.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 2.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Shift to P position/mode, and wait for at least 10 seconds.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 2.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9737: DTC P0962 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0962 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12343.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_c27a9b726436`
- Images: `images\GHH399856.jpeg`, `images\GHH399857.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19472.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0962 (K20C2)

DTC P0962: CVT Drive Pulley Pressure Control Valve Circuit Low

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the drive pulley. The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. The transmission control module (TCM) measures the current that passes through the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and uses feedback control to compensate for the difference between the measured current and the commanded one. The failure occurs by an open circuit or an intermittent open circuit. If the TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal or the measured current is not equal to the duty cycle command, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of the conditions is met for at least 1 second:

- TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal.

- The measured current for the TCM's command value is as specified here.

Duty (%) | Current (A) | Failure mode

56.5 - 90.2 | Less than 0.19 | Low Input

More than 90.2 | Less than 0.27 | Low Input

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve DRLS line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve internal circuit open

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Start the engine, and wait for at least 1 second.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P0971 (K20C2)

DTC P0971: Pressure Control Solenoid "C" Control Circuit High

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the forward clutch and the reverse brake. The CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. The transmission control module (TCM) measures the current that passes through the CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve and uses feedback control to compensate for the difference between the measured current and the commanded one. The failure occurs by a short circuit or an intermittent short circuit. If the TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal or the measured current is not equal to the duty cycle command, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of the conditions is met for at least 1 second:

- TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal.

- The measured current for the TCM's command value is as specified here.

Duty (%) | Current (A) | Failure mode

Less than 13.7 | More than 0.57 | High Input

13.7 - 27.4 | More than 0.88 | High Input

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve CPCLS line short

- CVT clutch pressure control solenoid valve internal circuit short

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Start the engine, and wait for at least 1 second.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9749: DTC P0976 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P0976 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12355.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_49913d2df4ce`
- Images: `images\GHH399880.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19484.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0976 (K20C2)

DTC P0976: Shift Solenoid Valve "B" Circuit Low

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Torque converter clutch status by shift solenoid valve B output

Torque Converter Clutch | Shift Solenoid Valve B

OFF | OFF

ON | ON

Shift solenoid valve B controls the drain circuit close/open commanded by the transmission control module (TCM) ON/OFF commands. Fluid passages which supply/discharge the hydraulic pressure to torque converter clutch is switched by shift solenoid valve B ON/OFF control. The TCM commands the driver circuit to turn on shift solenoid valve B. The circuit diagnoses malfunctions such as a circuit short or open, and sends back a return signal during the TCM's command. When the return signal does not match the TCM command to turn ON shift solenoid valve B, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The return signal does not match the command to turn ON shift solenoid valve B [Shift Sol B] for at least 1 second.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Shift solenoid valve B SOL B line short to ground

- Shift solenoid valve B internal circuit short

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine, and warm it up to normal operating temperature (the radiator fan comes on twice).

- Drive the vehicle in D position/mode at a steady speed of 37 mph (60 km/h), for at least 10 seconds.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9753: DTC P1717 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P1717 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12359.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_21b713f90ba1`
- Images: `images\GHH399884.jpeg`, `images\GHH399885.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19488.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P1717 (K20C2)

DTC P1717: Transmission Range Switch ATP RVS Switch (Open or Short)

General Description

Without paddle shifter

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

With paddle shifter

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Transmission range switch contact point input matrix

Without paddle shifter

Shift position/mode | Input per switch

ATP-P | ATP-R | ATP-RVS | ATP-N | ATP-D | ATP-S | ATP-L | ATP-FWD

P | O | X | X | X | X | X | X | X

R | X | O | O | X | X | X | X | X

N | X | X | X | O | X | X | X | X

Shift position/mode | Input per switch

ATP-P | ATP-R | ATP-RVS | ATP-N | ATP-D | ATP-S | ATP-L | ATP-FWD

D | X | X | X | X | O | X | X | O

S | X | X | X | X | X | O | X | O

L | X | X | X | X | X | X | O | O

With paddle shifter

Shift position/mode | Input per switch

ATP-P | ATP-R | ATP-RVS | ATP-N | ATP-D | ATP-S | ATP-FWD

P | O | X | X | X | X | X | X

R | X | O | O | X | X | X | X

N | X | X | X | O | X | X | X

D | X | X | X | X | O | X | O

S | X | X | X | X | X | O | O

O: Closed X: Open

The transmission range switch is attached to a control shaft. Operating the shift lever makes the control shaft rotate via the shift cable. The shift position indicator shows which position is selected according to the Low/High signal combinations, which vary based on the control shaft rotational angle. The control shaft changes the position of the transmission range switch, activates the manual valve, and switches hydraulic pressure to shift the transmission through forward/neutral/reverse. The transmission range switch signal is used to determine the shift schedule. The voltage is high (5 V or 12 V) at the transmission control module (TCM) input terminal when each transmission range switch position is open, and it is low (0 V) when each switch is closed. If the RVS switch is open with the shift lever in R position/mode while shifting between P, R, and N position/mode, the TCM detects an RVS switch open failure and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 320 milliseconds or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 11 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

No RVS signal [Reverse Switch(ATPRVS)] is detected with the shift lever in R position/mode while shifting between P, R, and N position/mode.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Transmission range switch ATP-RVS line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- Transmission range switch failure

- Shift cable adjustment failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, shift to P, R, through N position/mode, then shift to R position/mode again, and wait for at least 320 milliseconds.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9755: DTC P1840 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P1840 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12361.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P1840 (K20C2)

DTC P1840: CVT Speed Sensor Circuit Forward Rotation Range/Performance

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Tr1 | Tr2 | Pulse signal voltage level

OFF | OFF | VOH

OFF | ON | VOM

ON | OFF | VOL

The CVT speed sensor is attached to the outside of the transmission housing. The CVT speed sensor consists of a semiconductor to detect the position of the secondary driven gear. When the secondary driven gear rotates, the magnetic flux in the CVT speed sensor changes. The changes of magnetic flux are converted into pulsing signals to the transmission control module (TCM). The rotational direction of the secondary driven gear is detected by the voltage level of the pulsing signals. If the driving condition and the detected voltage level of the pulsing signals do not match, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 2 seconds or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Elapsed time after forward clutch engage completion | 3 seconds | -

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 500 rpm | -

Forward clutch commanded pressure | 295 kPa (3.0 kgf/cm 2, 42.7 psi) | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | 19 mph (30 km/h) | -

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

Drive pulley rotational direction [Direction of Drive Pulley Rotation] | Forward direction

Other | Not when the shift lever is being shifted

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The CVT speed sensor outputs [Direction of Secondary-shaft Rotation] a reverse direction signal for at least 2 seconds when driving in forward position.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT speed sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- Drive the vehicle in D position/mode at a speed of 19 mph (30 km/h) or more for at least 5 seconds.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 3.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC
````

````text
# DTC P1840 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

OFF | ON | VOM

The CVT speed sensor is attached to the outside of the torque converter housing. The CVT speed sensor consists of a semiconductor to detect the position of the secondary driven gear. When the secondary driven gear rotates, the magnetic flux in the CVT speed sensor changes. The changes of magnetic flux are converted into pulsing signals to the transmission control module (TCM). The rotational direction of the secondary driven gear is detected by the voltage level of the pulsing signals. If the driving condition and the detected voltage level of the pulsing signals do not match, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 2 seconds or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 500 rpm | -

Forward clutch commanded pressure | 295 kPa (3.0 kgf/cm 2, 42.7 psi) | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | 19 mph (30 km/h) | -

Shift lever position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

Drive pulley rotational direction [Direction of Drive Pulley Rotation] | Forward direction

Other | Not when the shift lever is being shifted

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The CVT speed sensor outputs [Direction of Secondary-shaft Rotation] a reverse direction signal for at least 2 seconds when driving in forward position.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT speed sensor failure

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Drive the vehicle in D position/mode at a speed of 19 mph (30 km/h) or more for at least 5 seconds.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 3.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.
````

## Chunk 9763: DTC P1855 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P1855 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12369.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_07c463fa7897`
- Images: `images\GHH399912.jpeg`, `images\GHH399913.jpeg`, `images\GHH399914.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19498.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P1855 (K20C2)

DTC P1855: Inclination Sensor Circuit Range/Performance

General Description

Without CAN gateway

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

With CAN gateway

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The G sensor is built into the SRS unit. The G sensor detects the road gradient at the time of vehicle stop and outputs information to the transmission control module (TCM). The TCM uses the G sensor output received by F-CAN lines for vehicle creeping torque switching. If the G sensor output voltage is less changed during the condition there is a forward and backward acceleration, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | Depending on the driving conditions

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator off

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Difference of front wheel right-and-left average speed and rear wheel right-and-left average speed | - | 3 mph (5 km/h)

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The G sensor output voltage change width is less than 0.03 V after the vehicle is driven at following conditions:

- Drive the vehicle at accelerator pedal position 1/16 or more and vehicle speed more than 4 mph (5 km/h) for at least 1 second.

- Reach the vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] to 14 mph (21 km/h) or more.

- Decelerate to more than 4 mph (5 km/h) with throttle fully closed for at least 1 second.

- Stop the vehicle.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- SRS unit internal failure (G sensor)

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever in D position/mode.

- Drive the vehicle at a speed of 14 mph (21 km/h) or more. (Accelerate for at least 1 second.)

- Release the accelerator pedal, and slow down the vehicle. (Take at least 1 second to decelerate to more than 4 mph (5 km/h).)

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 4.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on and the shift position indicator does not blink.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9764: DTC P1890 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P1890 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12370.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_87686227f195`
- Images: `images\GHH399915.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19499.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P1890 (K20C2)

DTC P1890: CVT Speed Control System

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The shift control system controls each gear position using a map stored in the transmission control module (TCM) memory. The shift control system controls the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve based on vehicle speed and the throttle position to keep the drive pulley speed close to the target rpm. It does this by varying the hydraulic pressure applied to the drive and driven pulleys and providing feedback on the difference between the target drive pulley speed and the actual drive pulley speed when driving. It supplies high hydraulic pressure to the driven pulley and enlarges the driven pulley belt diameter when shifting to a lower ratio. It supplies high hydraulic pressure to the drive pulley and enlarges the drive pulley belt diameter when shifting to a higher ratio. If the difference between the target drive pulley speed and the actual drive pulley speed falls within a certain range when in D, S, L, or R position/mode, the TCM detects a malfunction in the CVT shift function and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 18 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 576 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | 9 mph (14 km/h) | -

Accelerator pedal position | 4.7 deg. | -

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L, R

With paddle shifter | D, S, R

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The difference between the target drive pulley speed and the actual drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] is 500 rpm or more for at least 18 seconds.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT shift function failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Accelerate gradually to 37 mph (60 km/h) and drive at least 1 minute at 37 mph (60 km/h).

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the shift position indicator blinks and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P1890 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

DTC P1890: CVT Speed Control System

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The shift control system controls each gear position using a map stored in the transmission control module (TCM) memory. The shift control system controls the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve based on vehicle speed and the throttle position to keep the drive pulley speed close to the target rpm. It does this by varying the hydraulic pressure applied to the drive and driven pulleys and providing feedback on the difference between the target drive pulley speed and the actual drive pulley speed when driving. It supplies high hydraulic pressure to the driven pulley and enlarges the driven pulley belt diameter when shifting to a lower ratio. It supplies high hydraulic pressure to the drive pulley and enlarges the drive pulley belt diameter when shifting to a higher ratio. If the difference between the target drive pulley speed and the actual drive pulley speed falls within a certain range when in D, S, L, or R position/mode, the TCM detects a malfunction in the CVT shift function and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 18 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] | 576 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | 9 mph (14 km/h) | -

Accelerator pedal position | 4.7 deg. | -

Shift lever position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L, R

With paddle shifter | D, S, R

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The difference between the target drive pulley speed and the actual drive pulley speed [Input Shaft (Drive Pulley) Speed rpm] is 500 rpm or more for at least 18 seconds.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT shift function failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Accelerate gradually to 37 mph (60 km/h) and drive at least 1 minute at 37 mph (60 km/h).

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the shift position indicator blinks and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9766: DTC P1898 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC P1898 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12372.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_d7ea134a11d3`
- Images: `images\GHH399917.jpeg`, `images\GHH399918.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19501.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P1898 (K20C2)

DTC P1898: CVT Drive Pulley Pressure Control Valve Stuck ON or CVT Driven Pulley Pressure Control Valve Stuck OFF

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve control hydraulic pressure. The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve are controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. The transmission control module (TCM) measures the current that passes through the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve and uses feedback control to compensate for the difference between the measured current and the commanded one. To shift the transmission properly, the TCM outputs a drive/driven pulley hydraulic pressure control signal. If the pulley ratio is out of specified range from the vehicle stopped with the forward clutch engaged to the vehicle running, the TCM detects a CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck ON failure or a CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck OFF and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | Depending on the driving conditions

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | - | 3 mph (5 km/h)*

9 mph (13 km/h)** | -

Accelerator pedal position | 2.7 deg. | -

Shift lever position/mode | D

* : Symptom 1

**: Symptom 2

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of these symptoms occurs.

Symptom | Vehicle conditions | TCM detection

1 | Stopped with forward clutch engaged | Pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is more than 2, or less than 3 for at least 0.4 second

2 | Running | Pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is more than 1.8, or less than 3

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck on

- CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck off

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- Accelerate the vehicle in D position/mode until the speed reaches 37 mph (60 km/h).

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Accelerate the vehicle with wide open throttle.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 5.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P1899 (K20C2)

DTC P1899: CVT Drive Pulley Pressure Control Valve Stuck OFF or CVT Driven Pulley Pressure Control Valve Stuck ON

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve control hydraulic pressure. The CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve are controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. The transmission control module (TCM) measures the current that passes through the CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve and the CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve and uses feedback control to compensate for the difference between the measured current and the commanded one. To shift the transmission properly, the TCM outputs a drive/driven pulley hydraulic pressure control signal. If the pulley ratio is overdrive while the vehicle is stopped with the forward clutch engaged, and the estimated input torque when starting is a specified value or less and belt slippage does not occur when driving, the TCM detects a CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck OFF failure or a CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck ON and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | Depending on the driving conditions

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed]* | - | 3 mph (5 km/h)

Accelerator pedal position | - | 2.7 deg.*

4 deg.** | -

Shift lever position/mode | D

*: Symptom 1

**: Symptom 2

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of these conditions occurs:

- Both Symptom 1 and Symptom 2-A occurs.

- Both Symptom 1 and Symptom 2-B occurs.

Symptom | Vehicle conditions | TCM detection

1 | Stopped with forward clutch engaged | Pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is more than 0.3, or less than 0.6 for at least 2.5 seconds

2 | 2-A | Starting | Low input torque

2-B | Running | Pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is more than 3.0

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT drive pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck off

- CVT driven pulley pressure control solenoid valve stuck on

Confirmation Procedure

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- Accelerate the vehicle in D position/mode until the speed reaches 37 mph (60 km/h).

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Accelerate the vehicle with wide open throttle.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 5.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. # DTC P2715 (K20C2)
````

````text
DTC P2715: Pressure Control Solenoid "D" Stuck ON

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the torque converter clutch. The CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. To supply proper torque converter clutch hydraulic pressure, the transmission control module (TCM) outputs a CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve hydraulic pressure control signal. If the torque converter clutch is judged as stuck ON, the TCM detects a CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve stuck ON malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | Depending on the driving conditions

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 500 rpm | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed]* | - | 13 mph (21 km/h)

Acceleration** | - | 3.2 ft/s 2 (0.98 m/s 2)

Throttle position | 4 deg. | 42 deg.

Accelerator pedal position variation | - | 13 deg./1 second

Shift position/mode | Without paddle shifter | D, S, L

With paddle shifter | D, S

*: Symptom 1

**: Symptom 2

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

Symptom 2 occurs after Symptom 1 occurs.

- Symptom 1: Actual torque converter slip rate [ETR] is more than 99 %, or less than 130 % for at least 0.6 second when target torque converter slip rate is less than 97 %.

- Symptom 2: Actual torque converter slip rate [ETR] is more than 99 %, or less than 130 % for at least 1 second.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve stuck on

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever from N position/mode to D position/mode.

- Drive the vehicle at 12 mph (20 km/h) or less for at least 10 seconds.

- Slow down and stop the vehicle.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 4.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P2720 (K20C2)

DTC P2720: Pressure Control Solenoid "D" Control Circuit Low

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve controls hydraulic pressure to the torque converter clutch. The CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve is controlled by duty cycle output. The spool in the CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. The transmission control module (TCM) measures the current that passes through the CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve and uses feedback control to compensate for the difference between the measured current and the commanded one. The failure occurs by an open circuit or an intermittent open circuit. If the TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal or the measured current is not equal to the duty cycle command, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of the conditions is met for at least 1 second:

- TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal.

- The measured current for the TCM's command value is as specified here.

Duty (%) | Current (A) | Failure mode

56.5 - 90.2 | Less than 0.19 | Low Input

More than 90.2 | Less than 0.27 | Low Input

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve LCCLS line open (includes connector loose connection or disconnection)

- CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve internal circuit open

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Start the engine, and wait for at least 1 second.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The spool in the CVT lock-up clutch control solenoid valve moves the valve according to the current, and produces hydraulic pressure in proportion to the current. The failure occurs by an open circuit or an intermittent open circuit. If the TCM internal driver IC detects its abnormal or the measured current is not equal to the duty cycle command, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

- The measured current for the TCM's command value is as specified here.

Duty (%) | Current (A) | Failure mode

56.5 - 90.2 | Less than 0.19 | Low Input

More than 90.2 | Less than 0.27 | Low Input
````

## Chunk 9776: DTC P2817 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

- Title: DTC P2817 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)
- Source path: `pages\12382.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_514a13de819e`
- Images: `images\GHH399939.jpeg`, `images\GHH399940.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19511.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P2817 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

DTC P2817: Shift Solenoid Valve O/P (Pressure Control Solenoid "H") Stuck Off

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Transmission fluid pump volume changes by shift solenoid valve O/P ON/OFF control

Transmission fluid pump volume | Shift Solenoid Valve O/P

High | OFF

Low | ON

The shift solenoid valve O/P controls the drain circuit close/open commanded by the transmission control module (TCM) ON/OFF commands. The transmission fluid pump volume is changed by switching the shift solenoid valve O/P ON/OFF control. The TCM commands the driver circuit to turn on the shift solenoid valve O/P. The TCM temporarily increases the driven pulley pressure up to target value. As the commanded driven pulley pressure reaches the target value, the TCM monitors the driven pulley pressure when the shift solenoid valve O/P is ON and OFF. If the driven pulley pressure does not change, the TCM detects a shift solenoid valve O/P stuck OFF malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Once per driving cycle

Sequence | None

Duration | 10 seconds or less

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed] | 700 rpm | 750 rpm

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Transmission fluid temperature [CVTF Temperature] | 248 deg.F (120 deg.C) | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed] | - | 1 mph (3 km/h)

Shift lever position/mode | D

Other | Accelerator pedal released

Brake pedal pressed

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

Judgments 1 and 2 are executed sequentially, and if the driven pulley pressure does not change when the shift solenoid valve O/P [Shift Sol O/P] is ON and OFF.

Judgment | Shift solenoid valve O/P | Commanded driven pulley pressure | Driven pulley pressure | Detection time

1 | OFF | About 1, 961 kPa(20.0 kgf/cm 2, 284 psi) | About 1, 961 kPa(20.0 kgf/cm 2, 284 psi) | 5.0 seconds

2 | ON | About 1, 961 kPa(20.0 kgf/cm 2, 284 psi) | About 1, 961 kPa(20.0 kgf/cm 2, 284 psi) | 5.0 seconds

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Shift solenoid valve O/P stuck off

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever in from N position/mode to D position/mode.

- Accelerate the vehicle to 12 mph (20 km/h).

- Stop and leave the vehicle for at least 10 seconds.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 4.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P2818 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

DTC P2818: Shift Solenoid Valve O/P (Pressure Control Solenoid "H") Stuck On

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Transmission fluid pump volume changes by shift solenoid valve O/P ON/OFF control

Transmission fluid pump volume | Shift Solenoid Valve O/P

High | OFF

Low | ON

The shift solenoid valve O/P controls the drain circuit close/open commanded by the transmission control module (TCM) ON/OFF commands. The transmission fluid pump volume is changed by switching the shift solenoid valve O/P ON/OFF control. The TCM commands the driver circuit to turn on the shift solenoid valve O/P. The TCM temporarily increases the driven pulley pressure up to target value. As the commanded driven pulley pressure reaches the target value, the TCM monitors the driven pulley pressure when the shift solenoid valve O/P is ON and OFF. If the driven pulley pressure does not change, or the pulley ratio is at a specified value when decelerated to a stop, the TCM detects a shift solenoid valve O/P stuck ON malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Once per driving cycle

Sequence | None

Duration | 10 seconds or less

DTC Type | Two drive cycles, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Engine speed [Engine Speed]* | 700 rpm | 740 rpm

*: Symptom 1

** : Symptom 2

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Transmission fluid temperature [CVTF Temperature] | 248 deg.F (120 deg.C)* | -

194 deg.F (90 deg.C)** | -

Vehicle speed [Vehicle Speed]* | - | 1 mph (3 km/h)

Acceleration** | -11.1 ft/s 2 (-3.4 m/s 2) | -7.6 ft/s 2 (-2.3 m/s 2)

Shift lever position/mode | D

Other | Accelerator pedal released*

Brake pedal pressed

*: Symptom 1

** : Symptom 2

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of these symptoms occurs:

Symptom 1: Judgments 1 and 2 are executed sequentially, and if the driven pulley pressure does not change when the shift solenoid valve O/P [Shift Sol O/P] is ON and OFF.

Judgment | Shift solenoid valve O/P | Commanded driven pulley pressure | Driven pulley pressure | Detection time

1 | OFF | About 1, 961 kPa(20.0 kgf/cm 2, 284 psi) | About 1, 177 kPa(12.0 kgf/cm 2, 171 psi) | 5.0 seconds

2 | ON | About 1, 961 kPa(20.0 kgf/cm 2, 284 psi) | About 1, 177 kPa(12.0 kgf/cm 2, 171 psi) | 5.0 seconds

Symptom 2: The pulley ratio [Pulley Ratio] is less than 2.0 when the vehicle is stopped.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Shift solenoid valve O/P stuck on

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

- Start the engine.

- With the brake pedal pressed, place the shift lever in from N position/mode to D position/mode.

- Accelerate the vehicle to 25 mph (40 km/h).

- Stop and leave the vehicle for at least 10 seconds.

- Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

- Repeat Driving Pattern steps 1 through 4.

- Drive the vehicle in this manner only if the traffic regulations and ambient conditions allow.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected during the first drive cycle, a Pending DTC is stored in the TCM memory. If the malfunction returns in the next (second) drive cycle, the shift position indicator blinks and a Confirmed DTC and the freeze data are stored. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

````text
# DTC P281D (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY)

DTC P281D: Shift Solenoid Valve O/P (Pressure Control Solenoid "H") Control Circuit Low

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Transmission fluid pump volume changes by shift solenoid valve O/P ON/OFF control

Transmission fluid pump volume | Shift Solenoid Valve O/P

High | OFF

Low | ON

The shift solenoid valve O/P controls the drain circuit close/open commanded by the transmission control module (TCM) ON/OFF commands. The transmission fluid pump volume is changed by switching the shift solenoid valve O/P ON/OFF control. The TCM commands the driver circuit to turn on the shift solenoid valve O/P. The circuit diagnoses malfunctions such as a circuit short or open, and sends back a return signal during the TCM's command. When the return signal does not match the TCM command to turn ON the shift solenoid valve O/P, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The return signal does not match the command to turn ON shift solenoid valve O/P [Shift Sol O/P] for at least 1 second.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Shift solenoid valve O/P SOL C line short to ground

- Shift solenoid valve O/P internal circuit short

- TCM internal circuit failure

Confirmation Procedure

Operating Condition

- Start the engine.

- Shift the transmission to D position/mode while pressing the brake pedal, and wait for at least 30 seconds.

With the HDS

None.

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the shift position indicator blinks and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9780: DTC U0029 (K20C2 (CVT System))
