# DTC U0029 (K20C2 (CVT System))

DTC U0029: F-CAN Malfunction (F-CAN Bus OFF)

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The controller area network (CAN) transmits/receives pulsing signals to/from the control modules simultaneously by using two signal lines (F-CAN_H and F-CAN_L). When the transmission control module (TCM) cannot send the signals via the F-CAN lines for a set time, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

Elapsed time after the vehicle condition is turned to the ON mode | 3 seconds | -

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The TCM cannot send any signals via the F-CAN lines for at least 1 second.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line short to ground* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line short to ground* 2

- TCM internal circuit failure

*1: Without CAN gateway

*2: With CAN gateway

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

# DTC U0038 (K20C2 (CVT System))

DTC U0038: TM-CAN Malfunction (TCM-PCM)

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The transmission control module (TCM) and the powertrain control module (PCM) uses communication line (TM-CAN_H and TM-CAN_L) to transmit/receive multiple signals simultaneously. When the information is not sent from the PCM via the TM-CAN lines and this condition continues for a specified time, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1.5 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

The TCM does not receive any signals via the TM-CAN lines for at least 1.5 seconds.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- PCM failure (include fuse fall-outs)

- TM-CAN circuit TM-CAN_H line open

- TM-CAN circuit TM-CAN_L line open

- TM-CAN circuit TM-CAN_H line short to ground

- TM-CAN circuit TM-CAN_L line short to ground

- TCM internal circuit failure

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

# DTC U0100 (K20C2)

DTC U0100: F-CAN Malfunction (TCM - FI Control Module)

General Description

Without CAN gateway

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

With CAN gateway (12P connector type)

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

With CAN gateway (16P connector type)

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The controller area network (CAN) transmits/receives pulsing signals to/from the control modules simultaneously by using two signal lines (F-CAN_H and F-CAN_L). When the information is not sent from the powertrain control module (PCM) via the F-CAN lines and this condition continues for a specified time or when the information sent from the PCM is abnormal and this condition continues for a specified cycle, the transmission control module (TCM) detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1.5 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of these conditions is met:

- The TCM does not receive any signals from the PCM via F-CAN lines for at least 1.5 seconds.

- The information sent from the PCM is abnormal at least 20 times.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line short to ground* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line short to ground* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_H line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_L line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_H line short to ground* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_L line short to ground* 2

- PCM failure (include fuse fall-outs)

- TCM internal circuit failure

*1: Without CAN gateway

*2: With CAN gateway

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

Sequence | None

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_L line open* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_H line short to ground* 2

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN B_L line short to ground* 2

- VSA modulator-control unit failure (include fuse fall-outs)

- TCM internal circuit failure

*1: Without CAN gateway

*2: With CAN gateway

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the shift position indicator blinks and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.

- The information sent from the gauge control module is abnormal at least 20 times.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line open* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line open* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_H line open* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_L line open* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line short to ground* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line short to ground* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_H line short to ground* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_L line short to ground* 3

- Gauge control module failure (include fuse fall-outs)

- TCM internal circuit failure

*1: Without CAN gateway

*2: With CAN gateway (12P connector type)

*3: With CAN gateway (16P connector type)

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the shift position indicator blinks and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9792: DTC U0155 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY (CVT System))

- Title: DTC U0155 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY (CVT System))
- Source path: `pages\12398.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_b6640689730c`
- Images: `images\GHH399969.jpeg`, `images\GHH399970.jpeg`, `images\GHH399971.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19527.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC U0155 (L15B7/L15BA/L15BY (CVT System))

DTC U0155: Lost Communication With Gauge Control Module

General Description

Without CAN gateway

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

With CAN gateway (12P connector type)

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

With CAN gateway (16P connector type)

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The controller area network (CAN) transmits/receives pulsing signals to/from the control modules simultaneously by using two signal lines (F-CAN_H and F-CAN_L). When the information is not sent from the gauge control module via the F-CAN lines and this condition continues for a specified time or when the information sent from the gauge control module is abnormal and this condition continues for a specified cycle, the transmission control module (TCM) detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1.5 seconds or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL off, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition | Minimum | Maximum

12 volt battery voltage [Battery Voltage] | 10.0 V | -

[ ]: HDS Parameter

Malfunction Threshold

One of these conditions is met:

- The TCM does not receive any signals from the gauge control module via F-CAN lines for at least 1.5 seconds.

- The information sent from the gauge control module is abnormal at least 20 times.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line open* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line open* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line open* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_H line open* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_L line open* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_H line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN_L line short to ground* 1

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_H line short to ground* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN A_L line short to ground* 2/* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_H line short to ground* 3

- F-CAN circuit F-CAN C_L line short to ground* 3

- Gauge control module failure (include fuse fall-outs)

- TCM internal circuit failure

*1: Without CAN gateway

*2: With CAN gateway (12P connector type)

*3: With CAN gateway (16P connector type)

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the shift position indicator blinks and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory. The MIL does not come on.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9793: DTC U0302 (K20C2)

- Title: DTC U0302 (K20C2)
- Source path: `pages\12399.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_f655a96f1da5`
- Images: `images\GHH399972.jpeg`
- Duplicate sources: `pages\19528.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC U0302 (K20C2)

DTC U0302: PGM-FI System and A/T System Program Version Mismatch

General Description

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

The transmission control module (TCM) and the powertrain control module (PCM) uses communication line (TM-CAN_H and TM-CAN_L) to transmit/receive multiple signals simultaneously. When the software version in the TCM and software version sent from the PCM via the TM-CAN lines are mismatched (software version of the TCM is old compared to the PCM) for a set time, the TCM detects a malfunction and stores a DTC.

Monitor Execution, Sequence, Duration, DTC Type

Execution | Continuous

Sequence | None

Duration | 1 second or more

DTC Type | One drive cycle, MIL on, Shift position indicator blinks

Enable Conditions

Condition

Vehicle | ON mode

Malfunction Threshold

The software version in the TCM and software version sent from the PCM via the TM-CAN lines are mismatched (software version of the TCM is old compared to the PCM) for at least 1 second.

Possible Cause

NOTE: The causes shown may not be a complete list of all potential problems, and it is possible that there may be other causes.

- Software version of the TCM is old compared to the PCM

Diagnosis Details

Conditions for setting the DTC

When a malfunction is detected, the MIL comes on, the shift position indicator blinks, and a Pending DTC, a Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data are stored in the TCM memory.

Conditions for clearing the DTC

The shift position indicator comes off by turning the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. The MIL is cleared if the malfunction does not return in three consecutive trips in which the diagnostic runs. The MIL, the Pending DTC, the Confirmed DTC, and the freeze data can be cleared with the scan tool Clear command.
````

## Chunk 9795: DTC P0501 (K20C2 (CVT))

- Title: DTC P0501 (K20C2 (CVT))
- Source path: `pages\12401.html`
- Chunk ID: `chunk_924547787f20`
- Images: none
- Duplicate sources: `pages\15471.html`

### Full Text

````text
# DTC P0501 (K20C2 (CVT))

DTC P0501 : CVT Speed Sensor Circuit Range/Performance

NOTE:

- Before you troubleshoot, review the General Troubleshooting Information .

- This code is caused by an electrical circuit problem and cannot be caused by a mechanical problem in the transmission.

DTC Description | Confirmed DTC | Pending DTC | Freeze Frame

P0501 CVT Speed Sensor Circuit Range/Performance

DTC (CVT)

- Problem verification -1. Turn the vehicle to the ON mode. -2. Clear the DTC with the HDS. -3. Raise the vehicle on a lift. Make sure it is securely supported, and allow all four wheels to rotate freely. -4. Start the engine, disable the VSA by pressing the VSA OFF button. NOTE: Enter the VSA maintenance mode in case of the vehicle speed above 31 mph (50 km/h). -5. Run the vehicle with the shift lever in D position/mode, and the engine speed at 1, 000 rpm or higher, then release the accelerator pedal without pressing it again, for at least 4 seconds. Slow down and stop the wheels. -6. Check for Pending or Confirmed DTCs with the HDS. DTC Description Confirmed DTC Pending DTC Freeze Frame P0501 CVT Speed Sensor Circuit Range/Performance Is DTC P0501 indicated? YES The failure is duplicated. Go to step 2. NO Intermittent failure, the system is OK at this time. Check for poor connections and loose terminals between the CVT speed sensor and the TCM. If the freeze data/on-board snapshot of this DTC is recorded, try to reproduce the failure under the same conditions with the freeze data/on-board snapshot.

-1. Turn the vehicle to the ON mode.

-2. Clear the DTC with the HDS.

-3. Raise the vehicle on a lift. Make sure it is securely supported, and allow all four wheels to rotate freely.

-4. Start the engine, disable the VSA by pressing the VSA OFF button.

NOTE: Enter the VSA maintenance mode in case of the vehicle speed above 31 mph (50 km/h).

-5. Run the vehicle with the shift lever in D position/mode, and the engine speed at 1, 000 rpm or higher, then release the accelerator pedal without pressing it again, for at least 4 seconds. Slow down and stop the wheels.

-6. Check for Pending or Confirmed DTCs with the HDS.

DTC Description | Confirmed DTC | Pending DTC | Freeze Frame

P0501 CVT Speed Sensor Circuit Range/Performance

Is DTC P0501 indicated?

YES

The failure is duplicated. Go to step 2.

NO

Intermittent failure, the system is OK at this time. Check for poor connections and loose terminals between the CVT speed sensor and the TCM. If the freeze data/on-board snapshot of this DTC is recorded, try to reproduce the failure under the same conditions with the freeze data/on-board snapshot.

- CVT speed sensor installation check -1. Check for poor connections and loose terminals between the CVT speed sensor and the TCM, and check for proper installation of the CVT speed sensor. Are the connection and installation OK? YES Connection and installation are OK. Replace the CVT speed sensor . NO Repair faulty connection or installation.

-1. Check for poor connections and loose terminals between the CVT speed sensor and the TCM, and check for proper installation of the CVT speed sensor.

Are the connection and installation OK?

YES

Connection and installation are OK. Replace the CVT speed sensor .

NO

Repair faulty connection or installation.
````

-5. Run the vehicle with the shift lever in D position/mode, and the engine speed at 1, 000 rpm or higher, then release the accelerator pedal without pressing it again, for at least 4 seconds. Slow down and stop the wheels.

-6. Check for Pending or Confirmed DTCs with the HDS.

- Do not turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode while updating the TCM. If you do, the TCM can be damaged.
